## 2026-10-29 - Isolate disconnect logging and AutoModpack secret persistence

- User requested that the confirmed MineRENT connection incident fixes become part of `develop` for the next batch update with the other current features
- Bug / Server Sided / one closed player channel produced 21,417 repeated ServerLogin diagnostics, then AutoModpack synchronously persisted the reconnect secret on the server thread while MineRENT filesystem I/O stalled for more than 60 seconds and the watchdog stopped the fresh server / in testing
- JEM Compat now preserves the first `ClosedChannelException` diagnostic for each connection and cancels every duplicate regardless of the channel-open race
- JEM Bootstrap revision `jem.11` publishes each generated host secret to the current process immediately and performs the existing locked atomic persistence on one bounded daemon writer, so a slow host filesystem cannot block game ticks. Disk-backed multi-process lookup, player-bound secret replacement, expiry pruning and fail-closed handshake validation remain intact
- This change targets the server amplification after a real socket loss. It does not claim to repair the separate MineRENT route timeout to `ForeverSMP.minerent.io:25565`

## 2026-10-11 — Event inventory recovery, Chunky and Voxy load limits

- Пользователь напрямую запросил сохранять инвентарь после реальной смерти в активной структуре Boss Fight/Boss Raid и в Blood Moon, добавить Chunky, снизить зависания Voxy при запуске, ускоренной смене ночи и появлении modded structures, затем выполнить локальный test redeploy. Выбранная реализация: смерть и elimination остаются настоящими, но server-owned event lifecycle удерживает inventory, armor, offhand и Curios до respawn; Chunky становится явным операторским pre-generation инструментом, а Voxy/VSS получают ограниченный рабочий бюджет вместо конкуренции за все CPU/GPU/streaming ресурсы.
- Bug / Server Sided / после смерти участнику приходилось снова входить в опасную структуру за grave, потому что hosted encounters не сохраняли предметы, а Blood Moon сохраняла их только пока оставалась скрытая жизнь. Five whys: предметы оказывались в grave внутри арены → обычная grave-интеграция получала drops → hosted death не имела inventory snapshot → Blood Moon прекращала recovery на финальной смерти → сохранение предметов было ошибочно связано с продолжением попытки, а не с фактом участия в активном событии. Status: in testing. Механизм: живой active hosted participant сохраняет предметы только при смерти физически внутри canonical arena; accepted participant активной Blood Moon сохраняет их при каждой смерти, включая финальную elimination. Snapshot восстанавливается один раз после clone, grave/Curios drops подавляются только для этой смерти, служебный marker удаляется с обеих player entities, а смерть, respawn и провал события не отменяются.
- Bug / Both Sided / Voxy использовала дальность LOD 8192 блоков, 13 service threads и повышенный render pressure, тогда как VSS одновременно держала широкий streaming/prediction budget, а тяжёлые shader options усиливали всплески. Five whys: клиент надолго замирал при запуске, time skip и появлении structures → одновременно перестраивались LOD, освещение, predicted terrain и shader buffers → дальность и parallelism были настроены почти на максимум → VSS мог запрашивать и уточнять больше данных, чем нужно для ближайшего вида → client renderer и server generation конкурировали за CPU, disk, network и GPU без общего бюджета. Status: in testing. Механизм: Voxy ограничена радиусом 4096 блоков, четырьмя отдельными service threads и render pressure 1; VSS снижена до 64 chunks, bounded prediction/refinement, 4 Mbps client target и 750 KB/s server ceiling; дорогие Bloop reflections, RSM, shadow waves и high-quality water отключены, shadow/TAA/volumetric budgets снижены. Chunky 1.3.146 добавлен в clean Client и уже работает на Server для управляемой предварительной генерации реальных chunks; автоматический большой pregen не запускается, потому что он сам создаёт нагрузку и не заменяет client-side Voxy mesh/shader work.

## 2026-10-11 — Event cancellation, boss targeting and authoritative travel

- Пользователь сообщил, что до начала Boss Fight у Bosses Rise и, возможно, Cataclysm на большой дистанции самопроизвольно включаются boss-bar/music и агрессия, а во время боя смертельный удар иногда выглядит как отсутствие урона и переносит игрока к боссу. Пользователь отдельно подтвердил требование удалить весь переход «потеря жизни → половина здоровья → иммунитет → перенос к боссу»: умер означает умер. Выбранная реализация: общий hosted lobby становится единственным owner dormant AI, target, incoming combat и native presentation; фактическая смерть сразу переводит participant в `ELIMINATED`, не подменяя Minecraft death/respawn.
- Bug / Server Sided / `HostedLobby.hold` записывал только NBT timeout, но не останавливал AI, target или native presentation, а `StructureStaging` не переводил обычного живого structural boss в этот lobby при обнаружении. Five whys: boss-bar/music появлялись возле базы → Bosses Rise безусловно добавлял tracking player в `ServerBossEvent` и создавал music state при `startSeenByPlayer` → JEM не подавлял tracking-time presentation → hold не менял состояние entity → structural discovery только регистрировал arena/native anchor и ожидал уже начатую Party. Status: in testing. Механизм: любой registered structural boss вне active hosted encounter получает dormant hold; он очищает target/navigation, включает `NoAI`, отклоняет target changes и combat damage с участием босса в обе стороны, скрывает все принадлежащие entity `ServerBossEvent`, а для exact installed Bosses Rise 2.1.2 вызывает его public `setBarVisible(false)` и `setPlayingMusic(false)` также после StartTracking. Canonical Start восстанавливает presentation и AI одним release; exact installed Cataclysm 3.31 включает music только при живом player target, поэтому dormant target clear/deny заставляет его native tick рассылать music-off.
- Bug / Server Sided / `HostedEncounters.loseLife` отменял реальный `LivingDeathEvent` два первых раза, восстанавливал 50% HP, выдавал `reviveImmunityTicks` и телепортировал игрока в координаты босса, поэтому смертельный удар выглядел как случайный no-damage и принудительный boss pull. Five whys: игрок не умирал и оказывался у босса → death handler сознательно отменял смерть → hardcoded счётчик `LivesLost < 3` выдавал две скрытые жизни → восстановление было объединено с teleport и immunity → fallback lifecycle ошибочно подменял обычную смерть вместо маркировки результата попытки. Status: in testing. Механизм: death event больше не отменяется и не меняет здоровье/позицию; participant сразу становится `ELIMINATED`, после чего существующий terminal flow завершает провал при отсутствии оставшихся участников. Настоящий pre-death group revive остаётся отдельным lifecycle, но после фактической смерти скрытого продолжения нет.

Status: in testing. Механизм: существующий bounded ACTIVE loop назначает ближайшего живого `ALIVE` participant только когда допустимой цели нет; reload active hosted entity один раз восстанавливает combat `NoAI=false`. Failed cancellation до token validation не мутирует AI или encounter state; гипотеза о частичной отмене не подтвердилась.

Five whys: server tick мог завершиться `NullPointerException` → validator разыменовывал отсутствующего игрока → reacquisition перебирал все сохранённые UUID, включая offline → lookup штатно возвращал `null` для disconnected participant → новый target loop не повторил уже используемый в periodic-effects path offline filter. Status: in testing. Механизм: пользователь прямо запросил null check; Codex добавил его первым условием канонического `validTarget`, поэтому offline entry остаётся в lifecycle для disconnect grace, но безопасно исключается до проверки состояния, spectator flag, participant state, позиции и distance comparator.
- Bug / Both Sided / JEM перехватывал только death waypoint, а для остальных Xaero оставлял native operator teleport; boss discovery не имел отдельного server-authoritative travel request. Five whys: personal/home/base waypoint телепортировал → Xaero выполнял собственный coordinate command → JEM возвращал управление для любого не-death marker → сервер не получал authoritative destination ID → shared boss marker считался только presentation. Status: in testing. Механизм: на JEM dedicated connection native waypoint teleport action удаляется у personal/home/base/death markers; discovered boss отправляет только stable arena UUID, server требует system Landmark, canonical registered arena и Campaign eligibility, затем bounded queue грузит только target vicinity и выбирает safe external arrival. Integrated singleplayer и waypoint creation/display не меняются.
- Bug / Server Sided / structure safe-exit search начинался в одном block center от shell, поэтому край полного player box оставался менее чем в одном блоке от outer 0.25-block collision surface и hazard filter не исключал fire/magma/cactus/portals. Five whys: прибытие оказывалось вплотную к стене или опасному полу → reference был block center, а не effective wall + player box → первый perimeter offset равнялся одному → safety проверяла sturdy floor/fluid/collision, но не hazard set → map travel переиспользовал cleanup exit без отдельного arrival clearance contract. Status: in testing. Механизм: поиск начинается со второго внешнего block center, проверяет полный player box, загруженный chunk, world border, headroom, fluid и dangerous blocks; отсутствие точки отклоняет teleport вместо center/roof fallback.
- Локально исследованы exact installed `tpa-2.0.0.jar` и `BackToBed-Forge-1.20-1.1.0.jar`: TPA вызывает `ServerPlayer.teleportTo(ServerLevel, ..., Set, yaw, pitch)`, Back to Bed вызывает `Entity.teleportTo(DDD)` для игрока и mount, поэтому оба проходят уже существующий общий `BossTeleportSafety`; отдельные per-mod deny lists не добавлены. Сборки, gameplay/automated tests, server deployment и release promotion не выполнялись.

## 2026-10-02

02:00 — Development workspace and client delivery
Established `Client` as the single clean distribution source synchronized with the stable TLauncher profile, fixed an immutable SHA-256 release baseline, added cumulative point/full ZIP tooling with exact replacement and deletion manifests, reduced the project root to five working folders, and moved historical exports, reports, backups, generated builds and caches into a recoverable external archive.

02:00 — Living specifications
Added one current source of truth for Blood Moon, Boss Fight, Boss Raid and Resource Rush, a server terminal command reference, an SMP GUI interaction and visual-asset contract, and strict module ownership and native-asset-first rules for future client/server mod development.

00:01 — Encounter lifecycle and participants
Added one event-instance participant set with the host inserted independently of party membership, solo reduced to the host, preloaded arena chunks, resolved host and guest arrival slots, and deterministic PREPARING → TELEPORTING → ARRIVED → INTRO → ACTIVE startup.

00:01 — Blood Moon return and respawn
Recorded each participant's pre-event dimension, position and rotation once, added post-victory Stay/Return actions, and replaced center/corner respawn fallbacks with a validated central arena respawn point.

00:10 — Wave 3 and Wave 5 encounter balance
Removed all non-boss combat allies from Wave 3, removed Warden Stalkers entirely, reduced other Wave 5 auxiliaries from nineteen to six, slowed giant bosses by scale, and locked boss/add navigation during exact telegraph and impact windows.

00:10 — Boss presentation, scaling and attacks
Added reusable spawn presentations, canonical triggered Warden emergence, center-origin Nuclear Creeper arrival, full playable-floor propagation, phased WINDUP/TELEGRAPH/LOCKED_PATTERN/IMPACT/RECOVERY attacks, physical impact effects, and immutable shared attack timing/direction geometry. Replaced JEM hitbox, PoseStack, shadow and scale packets with Pehkui 3.8.2 as the single entity scale owner.

00:38 — Local server and Server Test runtime
Built and installed the updated JEM Server artifact with Pehkui 3.8.2 into the local server and JustEnoughMods Server Test profile, started both, and completed the localhost Forge handshake with the test player while leaving the stable JustEnoughMods profile and MineRENT unchanged.

Status: in testing. User direction: keep the approved visible full-height wall, leave it passable before combat, wait for every accepted participant, lock only active participants including mounted movement, keep outsiders fully passable and isolated, and release the wall for every ending or elimination. - 2026-10-09 — Bug / Client Sided / `ClientBootstrap.register()` invoked JEM SHA verification before AutoModpack could complete a first install, so a new client had no manifest, the precondition failed, and the normal consent/download/restart flow could never begin. Five whys: startup stopped because the manifest was missing; it was missing because first-install synchronization had not run; synchronization had not run because Forge registration executed the verifier first; the verifier ran first because JEM attempted to validate AutoModpack-managed output independently; that duplication existed because integrity auditing was incorrectly treated as a client bootstrap gate instead of AutoModpack transaction responsibility. Status: in testing. Resolution mechanism under test: remove the client verifier and version shortcut entirely while retaining a non-blocking server audit manifest.
- 2026-10-09 — Bug / Server Sided / rollback could be silently undone on the next restart because startup generation compared the restored journal head with the newer source tree and published that source again. Five whys: the restored content stopped being current because restart published another generation; restart published because `generate-modpack-on-start` was enabled; it was enabled to bootstrap an empty journal; that one-time requirement remained permanent because seeding and steady-state configuration were not separated; the lifecycle therefore treated recovery as ordinary source drift. Status: in testing. Resolution mechanism under test: perform one explicit initial seed, permanently disable startup generation, and load the journal head on every later start.
- 2026-10-09 — Bug / Server Sided / `/jempack` could remain unregistered even with AutoModpack loaded because Forge exposes the implementation as `automodpack_mod`, while the guard checked `automodpack`. Five whys: aliases were absent because their registration returned early; it returned because `ModList` reported the checked id as unloaded; the id differed because the bootstrap loads the Forge implementation module; JEM used the public product id instead of the runtime Forge id; the mismatch was not visible while commands had not been exercised on a live server. Status: in testing. Resolution mechanism under test: gate on `automodpack_mod` and delegate each alias through the native command dispatcher so native async errors remain visible.
- 2026-10-09 — Bug / Both Sided / publication could retain an untracked file manually introduced into the host layer and could include JEI bookmarks/history from a named server. Five whys: the extra paths survived because staging deleted only entries recorded in its previous state; manually introduced paths were absent from that state; they remained publishable because the host directory was not enforced as an exact mirror; JEI runtime history was also inside the broadly allowed `config` root; the original allowlist classified the root but not runtime-private subtrees. Status: in testing. Resolution mechanism under test: compare the complete host inventory against the clean Client allowlist on every stage and exclude `config/jei/world` at both staging and AutoModpack policy layers.
- 2026-10-08 — Bug / Server Sided / Невидимая physical shell зарегистрированной босс-арены продолжала существовать внутри территории активной Blood Moon: `structure_barrier` давал полную block collision мобам и неучастникам, а следующий server player tick дополнительно выталкивал любого неавторизованного игрока из structure bounds, поэтому свободно проходимая после начала боя граница Blood Moon фактически упиралась в другую арену и удерживала игроков и мобов. Five whys: пересечь участок было нельзя из-за collision shell и player boundary enforcement; оба механизма срабатывали потому, что босс-арена не знала об активной Blood Moon; она не знала о ней, потому что её shell синхронизировалась только по campaign lock и загруженным structure chunks; конфликт не устранялся окончанием подготовки Blood Moon, потому что арены имели независимых владельцев движения; независимость стала дефектом, когда их территории пересеклись, хотя Blood Moon уже является каноническим владельцем движения и состава мобов внутри своей активной территории. Status: in testing. User direction: исправить пересечение чисто на сервере, без обновления клиентов друзей. Codex independently suspends only the boss-arena shell that intersects the active Blood Moon and its player enforcement, keeps Blood Moon's existing event-mob containment and ambient-mob ejection authoritative, then restores the boss shell from the same bounds after the event through ordinary vanilla block synchronization.
- 2026-10-08 — User direction: MineRENT must support an explicitly requested server-only release when investigation proves friends' installed clients remain protocol- and registry-compatible. Codex independently split the deployment contract into normal client-and-server and exceptional proven server-only modes; the latter updates only server-owned artifacts and deliberately leaves the clean Client, stable profile, friend packages and client release baseline unchanged.
- 2026-10-08 — User direction: keep the rotten-flesh leather recipe, Immersive Aircraft sail repair, Digger toggle, and Progression anvil repair entirely server-side so friends retain their installed client. Codex independently preserved every existing registry and network contract, used synchronized server recipes, represented disabled Digger state in the existing item's NBT while removing/restoring the active enchantment for old-client visual consistency, and widened the existing Progression eligibility owner instead of adding a parallel anvil recipe.
- 2026-10-08 — Added a reusable `jem-server-only-update` Codex skill at the user's request. It requires exact installed-version and client/server-boundary investigation, prefers a correct server-authoritative seam, and stops with an explicit warning before any client artifact is changed when the requested behavior cannot remain server-only.
- 2026-10-08 — Bug / Server Sided / Immersive Aircraft 1.5.0 supplies `data/immersive_aircraft/recipes/sail.json`, but JEM's recipe replacement had historically removed every recipe whose output was `immersive_aircraft:sail`; merely deleting sail from that removal list stopped the explicit deletion but still left the final MineRENT recipe manager without the ID, as proved after the first deployment by `recipe give Sirio_o immersive_aircraft:sail` returning `Unknown recipe`. Five whys: the sail remained absent because the authoritative server recipe manager contained no `immersive_aircraft:sail`; the first fix did not create it because it only stopped one removal operation; stopping removal was insufficient because the final KubeJS/datapack recipe pass still produced no recipe under that ID; the implementation depended on an upstream recipe surviving every later transformation instead of asserting the required result; that dependency existed because the original replacement owner classified the sail only as a removable transport output rather than as a required biplane component. Status: in testing. Resolution mechanism under test: the server recipe owner now explicitly registers Immersive Aircraft's exact native 3×3 recipe under `immersive_aircraft:sail`, using six white carpets and three strings, so the synchronized recipe exists independently of datapack ordering without any client update.
- 2026-10-08 — Bug / Server Sided / A crafted `immersive_aircraft:sail` vanished from the player's hand because the server's Item Obliterator configuration still listed it in `blacklisted_items`, and `ServerPlayerMixin` consequently set the matching inventory stack count to zero during its per-slot player tick. Five whys: the stack vanished because its count was reset to zero; the reset ran because Item Obliterator classified the sail as disabled; it classified the sail as disabled because the exact item ID remained in the server blacklist; that blacklist survived because the recipe repair changed only the KubeJS recipe owner; the two independent exclusions existed because the sail had originally been disabled both as a recipe output and as a held item. Status: in testing. Resolution mechanism under test: remove only `immersive_aircraft:sail` from the server Item Obliterator blacklist and restart the dedicated server so its startup-loaded configuration accepts the item, while preserving every other disabled item and leaving all client distributions unchanged.
- 2026-10-08 — Bug / Server Sided / Progression anvil eligibility accepted base `ArmorItem` instances but rejected every modded armor subclass unless manually enumerated in `progression_armor_included`; the enchanted book reached vanilla's anvil loop, `canEnchant` returned false for that armor, and vanilla discarded the only proposed enchantment before producing an output. Five whys: the anvil output was empty because Progression was marked inapplicable; it was marked inapplicable because armor eligibility required the runtime class to equal `ArmorItem.class`; that equality excluded normal subclass-based mod armor; a tag partially compensated for it but required per-item enrollment; the manual allowlist contradicted the requested invariant that all armor is eligible. Status: in testing. Resolution mechanism under test: treat every non-excluded `ArmorItem` subclass as armor while preserving the existing attack-item predicates and explicit exclusions.
- 2026-10-08 — Bug / Server Sided / Digger had only an always-active block-break listener and no state stored on the enchanted stack; after the enchantment was present, every eligible break entered the 3×3 owner with no interaction capable of bypassing it. Five whys: players could not mine one block because the listener always saw an active enchantment; it always saw one because no enabled state existed; no state existed because the enchantment was implemented only as a passive level check; removing the enchantment permanently was the only prior escape; the missing owner was a server-side item interaction that preserves the enchantment for later restoration. Status: in testing. Resolution mechanism under test: sneak-right-click moves Digger between the active enchantment map and a disabled level stored on the same item, with a vanilla-localized actionbar state message.
- 2026-10-08 — Added the user-requested 1:1 server smelting recipe from rotten flesh to ordinary leather. Codex independently used the existing KubeJS server recipe owner so the dedicated server synchronizes it to unchanged clients.
- 2026-10-07 — Bug / Server Sided / Create Deployer activation loaded `JEMClaims`' shared reflective dispatcher, whose static initialization enumerated every declared method of `DeployerMovementBehaviour`; that enumeration forced the dedicated server to resolve the client-only `MultiBufferSource` parameter of `renderInContraption`, so `DeployerInvocation` failed before it could call the unrelated server-side activation method. Five whys: the server stopped because a ticking Deployer threw `NoClassDefFoundError`; it requested a client renderer class because reflection resolved every declared method signature; reflection enumerated them because the dispatcher searched only by method name; the dispatcher existed because inaccessible Create activation types were bypassed generically; the incompatibility reached MineRENT because this path activates only when a Create Deployer performs work. Status: in testing. The user supplied the MineRENT crash; Codex independently removed the reflective dispatcher and bracketed Create's own activation call with paired Mixin injections plus an exact field accessor, preserving nested machine-context cleanup and the existing claim-protection flow without invoking or reflecting over inaccessible Create methods.
